title: "Game Icons catalogue"
status: reference

# Game Icons catalogue

Generated from [game-icons/icons @ 82d948812bfe](https://github.com/game-icons/icons/tree/82d948812bfe3f269ef8f731dcdb07b08160edc4).

4180 icons. Names are exact, case-sensitive ligatures, including hyphens. Duplicate upstream filenames are contributor-prefixed (both variants).

Use `<i class="game-icons" aria-hidden="true">crystal-ball</i>`; bare `<i>` remains Material Symbols. Core Views may use this markup under the [View guide icon rules](#/gams-view-development-guide?section=icons).

See [build and licensing notes](#/game-icons-build). Previews below render the actual generated Game Icons font using the listed ligatures. Run `make serve-docs` to view them; no remote images are needed. Badges are repository tooling assets and are excluded, as in the upstream colorization script.

Icons made by the credited authors. CC BY 3.0 except rows marked CC0-1.0. The font removes the square backgrounds and converts artwork to monochrome glyphs with name ligatures. [Contributor notices](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/game-icons/icons/82d948812bfe3f269ef8f731dcdb07b08160edc4/license.txt).
